# Hezekai Wiki

访客入口由站点首页组件呈现。下方是 OKF 知识目录（与侧栏同源）。

The visitor entry is rendered by the site home component. The OKF knowledge directory follows (same catalog as the sidebar).


## 站内入口 / Site entry points

- [概览 / Overview](./overview.md) — 内容与页面组织。 / what the wiki holds and how pages are arranged.
- [关于与维护 / About and maintenance](./guides/about-and-maintenance.md) — 方法、双语约定与维护命令。 / method, bilingual convention, and maintainer commands.
- [编译页面 / Compiled pages](./concepts/compiled-pages.md) — Markdown 如何成为发布页。 / how a Markdown file becomes a published page.
- [添加页面 / Adding a page](./guides/adding-a-page.md) — 新页面放哪里。 / where a new page goes.
- [X 简报 / X digests](./sources/x-digest/index.md) — 每日精选归档。 / curated daily briefs archive.
- [日志 / Log](./log.md) — 入库时间线。 / filing timeline.
- [主题 / Topics](./topics/index.md) · [文章 / Articles](./sources/articles/index.md)

- [深度实测GPT-6.2 Astra，OpenAI在偏科的道路上越走越远了 / Deep Test of GPT-6.2 Astra: OpenAI Keeps Drifting Further Down the Path of Subject Bias](./sources/articles/deep-test-of-gpt-6-2-astra-openai-keeps-drifting-further-down-th.md) — 帖文对 GPT-6.2 Astra 与 Sol 的定价、能力评测及实测体验进行复盘，指出其在科研与代码审查上强势，但在沟通规划与审美生成上偏科。 / The post reviews GPT-6.2 Astra and Sol pricing, capability benchmarks, and hands-on testing, noting strengths in research and code review but subject bias in communication, planning, and aesthetic generation.

- [张立行 · Agent 把 CI 跑爆之后怎么省 / Zhang Lixing · How to Save CI Costs After Agents Run It Explosively](./sources/articles/zhang-lixing-how-to-save-ci-costs-after-agents-run-it-explosivel.md) — 张立行称，在 AI Agent 高频触发 CI 时，应先测量、减少无效运行、清理噪声测试，并通过缓存、分片与自托管跑机控制成本，加硬件为最后手段。 / Zhang Lixing says that when AI Agents trigger CI frequently, teams should measure first, reduce unnecessary runs, clean up noisy tests, and control costs via caching, sharding, and self-hosted runners, with adding hardware as a last resort.

- [收窄工具 / Constraining Tools](./sources/articles/constraining-tools.md) — Matt Pocock 提出通过极严格 lint 规则为智能体创建受限工具集的实验，暂定名为 /constrain。 / Matt Pocock proposes an experiment to create extremely constrained tool versions for agents using harsh lint rules, tentatively called /constrain.

- [评估 XState v6 Beta 的提示词 / Prompt for assessing XState v6 beta](./sources/articles/prompt-for-assessing-xstate-v6-beta.md) — Matt Pocock 发布一条用于评估 XState v6 Beta 的提示词，涵盖调研、概念验证、验证及多维度打分。 / Matt Pocock shares a prompt for assessing XState v6 beta, covering research, proof-of-concept, verification, and multi-dimensional scoring.

- [小墨：Cloudflare 为 Pi Agent 提供独立云端沙箱，需 Workers Paid / Xiao Mo: Cloudflare provides standalone cloud sandbox for Pi Agent, requires Workers Paid](./sources/articles/xiao-mo-cloudflare-provides-standalone-cloud-sandbox-for-pi-agen.md) — 帖文称 Cloudflare 已为 Pi Agent 准备独立云端 Linux 环境，实现隔离、多 Agent 并行与高自由度，但需开通每月 5 美元起的 Cloudflare Workers Paid。 / The post claims Cloudflare has prepared a standalone cloud Linux environment for Pi Agent, enabling isolation, multi-Agent concurrency, and high freedom, but requires Cloudflare Workers Paid starting at $5 per month.

- [外部沙箱为编程智能体提供安全执行环境 / External sandboxes for coding agents](./sources/articles/external-sandboxes-for-coding-agents.md) — 介绍三款可相互兼容的外部沙箱原型，让 Claude Code、Codex 与 Pi 等编程智能体在受控的 Linux 环境中运行命令。 / Introduces three compatible external sandbox prototypes that let coding agents such as Claude Code, Codex, and Pi run commands in controlled Linux environments.

- [西蒙·威利森介绍编程 Agent 的外部沙箱模式 / Simon Willison Introduces External Sandbox Pattern for Coding Agents](./sources/articles/simon-willison-introduces-external-sandbox-pattern-for-coding-ag.md) — 帖文称，代理可在 Mac 上运行，但将代码执行置于 WebAssembly、QEMU 或 Apple 容器沙箱中。 / The post says agents can run on a Mac while executing code in WebAssembly, QEMU, or Apple Container sandboxes.

- [介绍 Replit Missions：以智能体 swarm 承接复杂企业任务 / Introducing Replit Missions: Agent Swarms for Complex Enterprise Work](./sources/articles/introducing-replit-missions-agent-swarms-for-complex-enterprise.md) — Replit 推出 Missions，让团队在预算和边界控制下，将获客、性能优化、替代旧系统等复杂工作委托给智能体 swarm 执行。 / Replit launches Missions, letting teams delegate complex work such as enterprise sales, performance optimization, and legacy replacement to budget-bounded agent swarms.

- [Replit 发布 Missions 智能体群 harness / Replit Introduces Missions Agentic Swarm Harness](./sources/articles/replit-introduces-missions-agentic-swarm-harness.md) — Replit 推出 Missions，号称全球首个可在设定预算内完成任意目标的智能体群 harness。 / Replit launches Missions, billed as the world’s first agentic swarm harness to accomplish any goal within a set budget.

- [Claude 平台关联账单公测，支持通过组织付费且无需共享 API 密钥 / Claude Platform linked billing enters beta, pay via organization without API key](./sources/articles/claude-platform-linked-billing-enters-beta-pay-via-organization.md) — 帖文称 Claude 平台关联账单现已进入公测，Factory、Linear、Ramp 等工具用户可通过 Claude Platform 组织支付 Claude API 费用，无需共享 API 密钥。 / The post says Claude Platform linked billing is now in beta, letting users of tools like Factory, Linear, and Ramp pay for Claude API usage through their Claude Platform organization without sharing an API key.

- [Claude Code 代码模式（Code Mode） / Claude Code Code Mode](./sources/articles/claude-code-code-mode.md) — Claude Code 的实验性 Code Mode 让 Claude 编写 JavaScript，在一步中发起多个工具调用。 / Claude Code's experimental Code Mode lets Claude write JavaScript to make multiple tool calls in one step.

- [Claude Code 实验性 Code Mode 让 Claude 通过脚本串联工具 / Claude Code experimental Code Mode lets Claude chain tools via scripts](./sources/articles/claude-code-experimental-code-mode-lets-claude-chain-tools-via-s.md) — Claude Code 推出名为 Code Mode 的实验功能，Claude 可编写脚本串联工具并在长时间命令运行时继续回答。 / Claude Code launches an experimental Code Mode that lets Claude write scripts chaining tools together and keep responding while long commands run.

- [美国企业AI支出创新高，头部1%人均投入是典型企业625倍 / US Corporate AI Spending Hits Record, Top 1% Spends 625x More Per Employee Than Typical Firm](./sources/articles/us-corporate-ai-spending-hits-record-top-1-spends-625x-more-per.md) — 据Ramp数据，美国企业AI支出与采用率双创新高，头部1%企业人均AI投入远超中位企业。 / According to Ramp data, US corporate AI spending and adoption both hit records, with top 1% firms far outspending the median company per employee.

- [Levie：AI Token 每员工支出无上限，智能体将渗透更多知识工作 / Levie: AI Token Spend per Employee Has No Ceiling as Agents Spread Across Knowledge Work](./sources/articles/levie-ai-token-spend-per-employee-has-no-ceiling-as-agents-sprea.md) — Aaron Levie 认为 AI Token 每员工支出仍无上限，编码、计算机使用及智能体群等新形态将把自动化推向金融、生命科学、网安等更多企业知识工作领域。 / Aaron Levie argues AI token spend per employee has no ceiling, and coding, computer use, and agent swarms will push automation into more enterprise knowledge-work domains such as finance, life sciences, and cybersecurity.

- [把子 Agent 折进主循环：主循环优先于多子 Agent 架构 / Building agents: When to fold subagents into your main agent loop](./sources/articles/building-agents-when-to-fold-subagents-into-your-main-agent-loop.md) — 帖文称，在 Claude Platform 上构建 Agent 时，减少子 Agent、采用渐进式上下文与工具披露，往往能提升质量并降低成本与延迟。 / On Claude Platform, using fewer subagents with progressively disclosed context and tools can improve quality while reducing cost and latency.

- [埃森·莫利克：单一智能体常胜过企业专用子 Agent 碎片化 / Ethan Mollick: One Agent Often Beats Enterprise Specialized Subagent Sprawl](./sources/articles/ethan-mollick-one-agent-often-beats-enterprise-specialized-subag.md) — 莫利克转述 Anthropic 观点，称单一智能体通常比不同团队构建的专用子 Agent 更好、更便宜，并批评企业中泛滥的 Agentic 方案与 Harness。 / Mollick relays Anthropic's view that one agent often works better and cheaper than specialized subagents built by different teams, and criticizes sprawling agentic solutions and harnesses in enterprises.

- [Lambert · Qwen4-Max 仍是老策略 / Lambert · Qwen4-Max Still the Same Old Strategy](./sources/articles/lambert-qwen4-max-still-the-same-old-strategy.md) — Nathan Lambert 指出 Qwen 4 Max 仅将最强模型留作 API，下层仍有丰富开放权重，中国开源策略并未终结。 / Nathan Lambert says Qwen 4 Max keeps only the strongest model API-only while maintaining abundant open-weight models below; China's open-source strategy is not over.

- [Noam Brown · 更看好科学发现 / Noam Brown · More Bullish on Scientific Discovery](./sources/articles/noam-brown-more-bullish-on-scientific-discovery.md) — Noam Brown 称 GPT-6.2 Astra 每月 20 美元即可使用，并在部分领域超越顶尖人类智能，他最期待其带来的科学发现。 / Noam Brown says GPT-6.2 Astra costs $20/month, surpasses top human intelligence in some domains, and he is most excited about the scientific discoveries it may enable.

- [手感基准：GPT-6.2 Astra 与难以量化的模型品质 / Vibe Benchmarks: GPT-6.2 Astra and Hard-to-Quantify Model Qualities](./sources/articles/vibe-benchmarks-gpt-6-2-astra-and-hard-to-quantify-model-qualiti.md) — 帖文称早期测试者对 GPT-6.2 Astra 的评价印证了“手感基准”在数字指标之外的价值，因个性、毅力、创造力等品质虽难衡量却至关重要。 / The post says early tester reviews of GPT-6.2 Astra vindicate the value of vibe benchmarks beyond numeric metrics, since qualities like personality, perseverance, and creativity are hard to measure but matter.

- [外推：别因单次模型发布就过度判断 AI 走势 / Extrapolation: Don't over-judge AI trends from single model releases](./sources/articles/extrapolation-don-t-over-judge-ai-trends-from-single-model-relea.md) — 艾森·莫利克提醒，贴近 AI 新闻的人容易从每个新模型过度外推；模型人格比基准更重要，但基准无法捕捉。 / Ethan Mollick warns that those close to AI news tend to over-extrapolate from each new model, and model personality matters more than benchmarks can capture.

- [Astra 6.2 自主制作游戏：建模出色，却擅自添加离谱设定 / Astra 6.2 builds a game autonomously: strong 3D modeling, questionable self-added features](./sources/articles/astra-6-2-builds-a-game-autonomously-strong-3d-modeling-question.md) — Ethan Mollick 称 Astra 6.2 能在 Blender 和 Unity 中生成可玩游戏，但会不请自行加入不合逻辑内容，且难以控制。 / Ethan Mollick says Astra 6.2 can generate a playable game in Blender and Unity, but adds illogical content on its own and is difficult to control.

- [GPT-6.2 Astra 早期评测：难以引导却能完成宏大项目 / Early GPT-6.2 Astra Evaluation: Hard to Steer but Capable of Ambitious Projects](./sources/articles/early-gpt-6-2-astra-evaluation-hard-to-steer-but-capable-of-ambi.md) — Ethan Mollick 称在 GPT-6.2 Astra 上线前使用，发现其表现参差不齐、难以引导，但在处理从零用 Blender 与 Unity 制作太空电梯项目等宏大任务时表现出色。 / Ethan Mollick says that before GPT-6.2 Astra's launch, he found it uneven and harder to steer than other models, yet it delivered well on ambitious projects such as building a space elevator ride from scratch with Blender and Unity.

- [家族规模：训练数据指纹与蛋白质语言模型表现 / Family Size: Training Data Fingerprint and Protein Language Model Performance](./sources/articles/family-size-training-data-fingerprint-and-protein-language-model.md) — 帖文称，蛋白质家族越大，训练数据指纹越明显，Opus 5.5 与两类蛋白质语言模型随家族规模同步提升，而 GPT 与 Gemini 持平。 / The post claims larger protein families make training-data fingerprints easier to detect, with Opus 5.5 and two protein language models rising as family size grows, while GPT and Gemini stay flat.

- [Claude Fable 5.2 对比开源蛋白质语言模型 / Claude Fable 5.2 compared with open protein language models](./sources/articles/claude-fable-5-2-compared-with-open-protein-language-models.md) — 帖文称 Claude Fable 5.2 在相同输入下优于多款仅用蛋白质序列训练的开源蛋白质语言模型，Opus 5.5 与 ProtGPT3-10B 持平。 / The post claims Claude Fable 5.2 outperforms several open protein language models trained only on protein sequences under the same input, with Opus 5.5 level with ProtGPT3-10B.

- [DeepSeek chat-demo 开源三天，Star 已超过 3000 / DeepSeek chat-demo open-sourced for three days, stars exceed 3000](./sources/articles/deepseek-chat-demo-open-sourced-for-three-days-stars-exceed-3000.md) — DeepSeek 开源静态前端 chat-demo，让浏览器直接调用官方 API 实现搜索、读链接和语音等 App 同款能力。 / DeepSeek open-sources the static frontend chat-demo, letting the browser directly call the official API to deliver App-equivalent search, link reading, and voice capabilities.

- [同一模型换评分和配套，排行榜就可能翻面 / Same model, different scoring and harness, leaderboard conclusions may flip](./sources/articles/same-model-different-scoring-and-harness-leaderboard-conclusions.md) — 同一模型在不同评分规则和 Agent 配套下，评测排名和结论可能发生翻转。 / The same model can yield flipped leaderboard rankings and conclusions under different scoring rules and Agent harnesses.

- [No AI Slop 宣言 / No AI Slop Manifesto](./sources/articles/no-ai-slop-manifesto.md) — AI 工程师峰会参与者联合发布宣言，区分负责任的 AI 使用与低劣 slop，强调人类判断与审核不可外包。 / AI Engineer Summit participants release the No AI Slop Manifesto, distinguishing responsible AI use from low-effort slop and stressing that human judgment and review cannot be outsourced.

- [No AI Slop：反 AI 滥制内容宣言 / No AI Slop: A Manifesto](./sources/articles/no-ai-slop-a-manifesto.md) — 一份由 AIE Code Summit 2026 参与者发起的宣言，主张在 AI 辅助开发中坚持人类判断与审核，反对低质量 AI 产出（slop）。 / A manifesto launched by AIE Code Summit 2026 participants advocating human judgment and review in AI-assisted development, opposing low-quality AI output ("slop").

- [四个神经元，怎么学会追光 / How Four Neurons Learn to Chase Light](./sources/articles/how-four-neurons-learn-to-chase-light.md) — 帖文介绍《Watch a Brain Learn》中仅用四个神经元模拟大脑学习追光、逃跑等行为的过程。 / The post introduces how a four-neuron simulation in "Watch a Brain Learn" learns to chase light, escape, and other behaviors through reward and punishment.

- [观察大脑如何学习 / Watch a Brain Learn](./sources/articles/watch-a-brain-learn.md) — 一篇可视化强化学习文章，展示只有四个神经元的数字生物如何通过试错、奖励与惩罚自主学习寻光。 / A visual reinforcement-learning article showing how a four-neuron digital creature autonomously learns to seek light through trial, error, reward, and punishment.

- [主会话加 Thread，Token 其实没那么吓人 / Main Session Plus Thread: Tokens Are Not That Scary](./sources/articles/main-session-plus-thread-tokens-are-not-that-scary.md) — 帖文称，通过主会话路由与子会话专用模型，配合 Prompt Caching 和上下文压缩，可降低 Agent Token 消耗。 / The post claims that main-session routing with dedicated sub-session models, plus Prompt Caching and context compression, can reduce Agent token consumption.

- [AI 手搓几百K纯 HTML 3D 世界，让 HTML 再次伟大 / AI Hand-Crafted Hundred-KB Pure HTML 3D Worlds Make HTML Great Again](./sources/articles/ai-hand-crafted-hundred-kb-pure-html-3d-worlds-make-html-great-a.md) — 帖文称，AI 生成的极小体积纯 HTML 3D 游戏依靠 Three.js 与程序化生成，在浏览器中现场渲染出近 3A 级画面。 / The post claims AI-generated ultra-small pure HTML 3D games rely on Three.js and procedural generation to render near-3A visuals in the browser on the fly.

- [最爱用的 10 句 Agent Prompt / Top 10 Favorite Agent Prompts](./sources/articles/top-10-favorite-agent-prompts.md) — 一位用户在 X 上分享近两个月与 Agent 协作最常使用的 10 条提示词，涵盖根因分析、方案对齐、二阶影响推演等工程实践。 / A user on X shares 10 favorite prompts used when collaborating with Agents over the past two months, covering root-cause analysis, option alignment, second-order impact analysis, and other engineering practices.

- [软件工厂的秘密：让 Agent 抱怨产品与代码库 / The secret to the software factory: make agents complain about your product and codebase](./sources/articles/the-secret-to-the-software-factory-make-agents-complain-about-yo.md) — Kent C. Dodds 称，软件工厂的秘诀是让智能体抱怨在你的产品或代码库中工作时的痛点。 / Kent C. Dodds says the secret to the software factory is getting agents to complain about what they hate about working in your product or codebase.

- [少发提示 / Send Fewer Prompts](./sources/articles/send-fewer-prompts.md) — 下次启动 Agent 会话或发送提示词前，先自问如何无需亲自发送也能得到相同结果，这就是构建软件工厂的方法。 / Before starting an agent session or sending a prompt, ask how to get the same outcome without sending it yourself—that's how you build a software factory.

- [No AI Slop Manifesto：人类判断不可被 AI 取代 / No AI Slop Manifesto: Human Judgment Cannot Be Replaced by AI](./sources/articles/no-ai-slop-manifesto-human-judgment-cannot-be-replaced-by-ai.md) — 帖文称《No AI Slop Manifesto》已获 300 多签名，主张 AI 仅能放大人类判断，出品方需对意图、品味与后果负责。 / The post says the 'No AI Slop Manifesto' has 300+ signatures and argues AI can only amplify human judgment, while shippers own intent, taste, and consequences.

- [Matt · /animatic / Matt · /animatic](./sources/articles/matt-animatic.md) — Matt Pocock 介绍两个用于生成可上传视频的 /animatic 技能及其设置工具。 / Matt Pocock introduces two /animatic skills for generating upload-ready videos and their setup tool.

- [Vox · 过夜任务别卡死在一步 / Vox · Don't let overnight tasks get stuck on one step](./sources/articles/vox-don-t-let-overnight-tasks-get-stuck-on-one-step.md) — 用 Herdr 让 Claude Opus 与 GPT-6.1 Sol 互审，通过 CLAUDE.md 分步验证实现过夜自动编程 / Use Herdr to let Claude Opus and GPT-6.1 Sol review each other, achieving overnight automatic programming through step-by-step verification in CLAUDE.md

- [Vox · 预览链接直接开到手机上 / Vox · Preview links open directly on phone](./sources/articles/vox-preview-links-open-directly-on-phone.md) — 通过 Tailscale 让 AI 编程工具生成的预览链接直接在手机上打开 / Use Tailscale so preview links from AI coding tools open directly on your phone

- [六段提示：把 Fable 5.2 当实习生用 / Six Prompts: Use Fable 5.2 Like an Intern](./sources/articles/six-prompts-use-fable-5-2-like-an-intern.md) — 帖文列出六段可复制粘贴的提示，建议把 Fable 5.2 当实习生而非魔法工具来使用。 / The post lists six copy-paste prompts and advises treating Fable 5.2 like an intern rather than a magic tool.

Fable, Opus, Sonnet, or Haiku](./sources/articles/which-claude-model-should-you-build-with-fable-opus-sonnet-or-ha.md) — 官方文档解读如何在 Claude Haiku、Sonnet、Opus 与 Fable 之间根据任务类型、成本与延迟选择模型。 / An official guide to choosing among Claude Haiku, Sonnet, Opus, and Fable models based on task type, cost, and latency.

- [Addy Osmani：先加思考再换模型——Opus 5.5 日常够用，Fable 5.2 留给硬骨头 / Addy Osmani: Raise Effort Before Switching Models — Opus 5.5 for Daily Coding, Fable 5.2 for Hard Tasks](./sources/articles/addy-osmani-raise-effort-before-switching-models-opus-5-5-for-da.md) — Addy Osmani 建议以 Opus 5.5 medium 为编码日常主力，遇阻先调高 effort，仅在大型迁移、长时无人值守等真正困难任务上才上 Fable 5.2；Garry Tan 实测显示 Opus 解决率略高且单任务成本仅为 Fable 的 1/2.8。 / Addy Osmani recommends Opus 5.5 at medium as the daily coding driver, raising effort before switching models, and reserving Fable 5.2 for genuinely hard tasks like big migrations; Garry Tan's benchmarks show Opus solves slightly more tasks at 1/2.8 the cost per solve.

- [Mitchell · 别做 demoslop / Mitchell · Don't Make Demoslop](./sources/articles/mitchell-don-t-make-demoslop.md) — 米切尔表示 Superlogical 厌倦了像 demoslop 一样的软件，致力于展示精心打磨的软件，并强调用经验丰富的工匠配合 AI，而非无趣的提示词。 / Mitchell says Superlogical is tired of software that feels like demoslop and aims to show meticulously crafted software, using experienced craftspeople enhanced by AI rather than tasteless prompting.

- [达尔文.skill 3.0 发布：改用实战盲评替代文档投票 / Darwin.skill 3.0 Released: Replacing Document Voting with Task-Based Blind Evaluation](./sources/articles/darwin-skill-3-0-released-replacing-document-voting-with-task-ba.md) — 达尔文.skill 3.0 重构评测方式，让两版技能各执行任务后由评委盲评产出并由脚本裁决，平局保留更短版本。 / Darwin.skill 3.0 rebuilds evaluation by having two skill versions execute tasks, with judges blind-scoring outputs and a script adjudicating, keeping the shorter version on ties.

- [斯坦福 CS146S 客座讲座：Agent 优先世界中的安全编码 / Stanford CS146S Guest Lecture: Secure Coding in an Agent-First World](./sources/articles/stanford-cs146s-guest-lecture-secure-coding-in-an-agent-first-wo.md) — Semgrep 联合创始人兼 CEO Isaac Evans 在斯坦福 CS146S 做客座讲座，发布 47 页讲义《Agent 优先世界中的安全编码》，主张让利用软件漏洞变得更贵。 / Semgrep co-founder and CEO Isaac Evans gave a guest lecture at Stanford CS146S, releasing a 47-page handout "Secure Coding in an Agent-First World," advocating making software vulnerability exploitation more expensive.

- [Semgrep CEO 斯坦福客座讲座：Agent 时代让攻击变得更贵 / Semgrep CEO Stanford Guest Lecture: Make Attacks More Expensive in the Agent Era](./sources/articles/semgrep-ceo-stanford-guest-lecture-make-attacks-more-expensive-i.md) — Semgrep 联合创始人兼 CEO Isaac Evans 在斯坦福 CS146S 讲座中主张，在 Agent 先写代码的时代，漏洞修不完就让攻击变得更贵，并提出建立“安全工厂”。 / Semgrep co-founder and CEO Isaac Evans argues in a Stanford CS146S lecture that in an agent-first coding era, since vulnerabilities cannot all be fixed, attacks should be made more expensive, proposing a “security factory.”

- [Grok Bot 做客 YC Lightcone：AI 同事访谈与身份治理边界 / Grok Bot on YC Lightcone: AI Teammate Interview and Identity Governance Boundaries](./sources/articles/grok-bot-on-yc-lightcone-ai-teammate-interview-and-identity-gove.md) — SpaceXSI 核心成员在 YC Lightcone 分享 Grok Bot 七周从代码到上线，探讨以 Bot 为基本单位的 AI 同事工作方式及其身份共享与治理边界。 / SpaceXSI core members share on YC Lightcone how Grok Bot went from code to launch in seven weeks, exploring an AI teammate work model with Bot as the basic unit and its identity sharing and governance boundaries.

- [读 Claude「Building always-on agents」的五个要点 / Five takeaways from Claude's "Building always-on agents"](./sources/articles/five-takeaways-from-claude-s-building-always-on-agents.md) — 帖文梳理了 Anthropic 始终在线 Agent 架构的五个要点：收件箱、计时器、记忆、权限与发件箱，并指出始终在线是体验而非一直开机。 / The post distills five takeaways from Anthropic's always-on agent architecture—inbox, timers, memory, permissions, and outbox—and stresses that always-on is an experience, not constant uptime.

- [Vox · 提前压缩上下文 / Vox · Early Context Compression](./sources/articles/vox-early-context-compression.md) — 在 Claude Code 长对话中提前设置自动压缩点，可降低 Token 使用量。 / Set an earlier auto-compact point in Claude Code long conversations to reduce token usage.

- [Vox · 清理理想路径债 / Vox · Clearing Happy-Path Debt](./sources/articles/vox-clearing-happy-path-debt.md) — AI 编程易只构建理想路径，需补全缺失状态与布局异常，且不改设计、不破坏功能。 / AI coding tends to build only the ideal path; fix missing states and layout breaks without changing design or breaking functionality.

- [Vox · 删注释 / Vox · Delete Comments](./sources/articles/vox-delete-comments.md) — 建议在 AI 代理使用的代码库中删除大部分注释，防止代理把过时注释和临时变通当作可复制模式。 / Recommend deleting most comments in codebases used by AI agents to prevent them from copying stale comments and temporary workarounds as patterns.

- [Matt · Home Repo 模式 / Matt · Home Repo Pattern](./sources/articles/matt-home-repo-pattern.md) — Matt Pocock 介绍 Home Repo 模式，作为在多个仓库中运行 Agent 的简单方案，并提供可在组织仓库上运行的设置提示词。 / Matt Pocock introduces the Home Repo pattern, a simple solution for running agents across multiple repositories, with a prompt to set it up on an organization's repos.

- [AI 对普通人最大的优势不是更快 / AI's biggest advantage for ordinary people is not speed](./sources/articles/ai-s-biggest-advantage-for-ordinary-people-is-not-speed.md) — 帖文称 AI 对普通人的最大价值不在于加速产品交付，而在于让普通人和小团队能用数据方法论与工作流构建增长飞轮，像字节跳动一样管理产品与商业逻辑。 / The post claims AI's greatest value for ordinary people is not accelerating product delivery, but enabling ordinary people and small teams to build growth flywheels using data methodology and workflows, managing products and business logic like ByteDance.

- [豆包工作接入 DeepSeek、Kimi、智谱 GLM 等三方模型 / Doubao Work integrates third-party models including DeepSeek, Kimi, and Zhipu GLM](./sources/articles/doubao-work-integrates-third-party-models-including-deepseek-kim.md) — 豆包工作终于接入三方模型，新用户可领30天会员，自动模式下简单任务免费，Doubao 2.1 Lite和DeepSeek V4.1 Flash倍率0.01。 / Doubao Work finally integrates third-party models; new users can claim a 30-day membership, automatic mode makes simple tasks free, and Doubao 2.1 Lite and DeepSeek V4.1 Flash are priced at a 0.01 multiplier.

- [想用 skill 加 DeepSeek Harness 复刻 Fable / Recreating Fable with Skill and DeepSeek Harness](./sources/articles/recreating-fable-with-skill-and-deepseek-harness.md) — 用户称用 Skill 和 DeepSeek Harness 复刻 Fable 5.2，一下午用 45 亿 token 生成 30MB HTML 3D 动作游戏 / User claims to recreate Fable 5.2 using Skill and DeepSeek Harness, generating a 30 MB HTML 3D action game in one afternoon with 45 billion tokens

- [Fable 5.2 3D 游戏优化可整合进单一 HTML，4399 或迎 HTML 游戏分发复兴 / Fable 5.2 3D game optimizations pack into one HTML; 4399 may see HTML game distribution revival](./sources/articles/fable-5-2-3d-game-optimizations-pack-into-one-html-4399-may-see.md) — 帖文称 Fable 5.2 的 3D 游戏优化可整合进单一 HTML，网页发布最便捷且跨平台，4399 有望迎来以 HTML 为基础的游戏分发平台复兴。 / The post claims Fable 5.2's 3D game optimizations can be packed into one HTML, web publishing is the most convenient and cross-platform method, and 4399 may revive with an HTML-based game distribution platform.

- [网页游戏回归与 AI 驱动的游戏开发新范式 / Web Games Return and AI-Driven Game Development Paradigm](./sources/articles/web-games-return-and-ai-driven-game-development-paradigm.md) — 帖文称网页游戏凭借免安装、低门槛分发正回归，AI 工具可在一天内生成 3D 游戏，预示 HTML 游戏分发平台机会。 / The post claims web games are returning due to no-install, low-barrier distribution, and AI tools can generate 3D games in a day, signaling opportunities for HTML game distribution platforms.

- [试玩「魂玉0」AI 小游戏 / Playtest: "Hun Yu 0" AI Mini-game](./sources/articles/playtest-hun-yu-0-ai-mini-game.md) — 一款由 AI 使用 guizang-game-skill 制作、基于 three.js 的 WebGL 2 试玩小游戏。 / A WebGL 2 playtest mini-game made by AI using guizang-game-skill and built on three.js.

- [一下午做出 3D 动作游戏「雾锁山门」 / 3D Action Game "Mist-Locked Mountain Gate" Made in One Afternoon](./sources/articles/3d-action-game-mist-locked-mountain-gate-made-in-one-afternoon.md) — 帖文称 Fable 5.2 用一下午、45 亿 token，把模型、场景、动作与优化都做进一个 30 MB HTML 文件，完成了 3D 动作游戏「雾锁山门」。 / The post claims Fable 5.2 built the 3D action game "Mist-Locked Mountain Gate" in one afternoon using 4.5 billion tokens, bundling models, scenes, actions, and optimization into a single 30 MB HTML file.

- [三条路线：Pi Agent、DeepSeek Harness 与 Qwen Code 1.0 的架构取舍 / Three Routes: Architecture Trade-offs Among Pi Agent, DeepSeek Harness, and Qwen Code 1.0](./sources/articles/three-routes-architecture-trade-offs-among-pi-agent-deepseek-har.md) — 帖文对比 Pi Agent、DeepSeek Harness 与 Qwen Code 1.0 三种 Agent 架构路线，并称 Qwen Code 可对接 Grok 等多种模型。 / The post compares three Agent architecture routes—Pi Agent, DeepSeek Harness, and Qwen Code 1.0—and claims Qwen Code can connect to models such as Grok.

- [PiClaw：基于 Pi Agent 的可插件个人 AI 助手工作台 / PiClaw: Plugin-based Personal AI Assistant Workbench Built on Pi Agent](./sources/articles/piclaw-plugin-based-personal-ai-assistant-workbench-built-on-pi.md) — PiClaw 在 Pi Agent 之上搭建 Web 工作台，支持插件扩展、长期记忆与 Docker 自托管，将编程 Agent 转化为个人助手。 / PiClaw builds a web workbench on Pi Agent, supporting plugin extensions, long-term memory, and Docker self-hosting to turn a coding agent into a personal assistant.

- [AI 的答案是工程 / The answer to AI is engineering](./sources/articles/the-answer-to-ai-is-engineering.md) — Geoffrey Huntley 在 AI DevCon New York 的演讲称，面对安全接管新常态，答案不是更多 AI，而是工程；他主张用 Nix Overlay 建立单一事实来源，对 Git 等软件打猴子补丁，并减少对开源库依赖。 / Geoffrey Huntley's AI DevCon New York talk argues that security take-off is the new normal and the answer to AI is engineering, not more AI; he advocates Nix overlays as a single source of truth to monkey-patch tools like Git and reduce reliance on open-source libraries.

- [Huntley · 安全才是瓶颈 / Huntley · Security Is the New Bottleneck](./sources/articles/huntley-security-is-the-new-bottleneck.md) — Geoffrey Huntley 称安全已成工程瓶颈，主张以 Nix 环境统一、Agent 自动化补丁与确定性检查取代 AI 炒作。 / Geoffrey Huntley says security has become the engineering bottleneck, advocating unified Nix environments, agent-automated patching, and deterministic checks over AI hype.

- [Fable 5.2 代码审查实测：high 档位优于 xhigh / Fable 5.2 Code Review Real-World Test: high Effort Beats xhigh](./sources/articles/fable-5-2-code-review-real-world-test-high-effort-beats-xhigh.md) — 帖文称 Fable 5.2 在 Claude Code 中以 high 动力审代码效果最优，xhigh 虽更耗 token 却发现更少问题。 / The post claims Fable 5.2 reviews code best on Claude Code's high effort setting, while xhigh burns more tokens yet finds fewer issues.

- [清规则：用 Anthropic Fable 5.2 指南审查过时项目指令 / Clear Rules: Review Outdated Project Instructions with Anthropic's Fable 5.2 Prompting Guide](./sources/articles/clear-rules-review-outdated-project-instructions-with-anthropic.md) — Anthropic 发布 Fable 5.2 提示词指南，建议复审 CLAUDE.md、AGENTS.md 等旧指令，防止过时规则让新模型浪费 Token。 / Anthropic released a Fable 5.2 prompting guide advising a review of outdated project instructions such as CLAUDE.md and AGENTS.md to prevent legacy rules from making the new model waste tokens.

- [流程即软件 / Process Is Software](./sources/articles/process-is-software.md) — 帖文称，用清晰一致的语言描述业务流程，就已经构建了一半自动化所需软件，DDD 从未如此强大。 / The post says describing business processes in clear, consistent language means half the automation software is already built, and DDD has never been more powerful.

- [领域语言：DDD 与智能体协作的核心 / Domain Language: DDD and Agent Collaboration](./sources/articles/domain-language-ddd-and-agent-collaboration.md) — 帖文称，马特·波科克课程中最令学员惊讶的不是智能体功能，而是领域驱动设计中的通用语言及其与智能体协作和代码重构。 / The post states that the most surprising part of Matt Pocock's course is not agentic features, but domain-driven design's ubiquitous language and its use with agents and code refactoring.

- [语言：Rust 与 Agent 时代的新编程语言 / Language: Rust and New Programming Languages in the Agent Era](./sources/articles/language-rust-and-new-programming-languages-in-the-agent-era.md) — Guillermo Rauch 认为 Rust 优秀，但 Agent 时代将催生更多专用语言，AI 让工具不再一刀切。 / Guillermo Rauch considers Rust superb, but expects the agent era to spawn more purpose-built languages, with AI making tooling fluid rather than one-size-fits-all.

- [创造优秀产品仍需非凡专注 / Creating Wonderful Products Still Requires Extraordinary Human Focus](./sources/articles/creating-wonderful-products-still-requires-extraordinary-human-f.md) — 吉列尔莫·劳奇发帖提醒，打造优秀产品依然需要非凡的人类专注、努力、动机、爱与注意力。 / Guillermo Rauch posts that building wonderful products still requires extraordinary human focus, effort, motivation, love, and attention span.

- [Chase · 目标工程 / Chase · Goal Engineering](./sources/articles/chase-goal-engineering.md) — Harrison Chase 在 X 上发帖，以删除线划掉“上下文工程”与“工程链条”，提出“目标工程”。 / Harrison Chase posts on X, striking through “context engineering” and “harness engineering” to propose “goal engineering.”

- [Levie · Agent 蜂群 / Levie · Agent Swarms](./sources/articles/levie-agent-swarms.md) — Aaron Levie 认为 Agent 蜂群将推动 AI 产出再次跃升，并拉开采用该模式的团队与未采用团队的差距。 / Aaron Levie argues agent swarms will drive another leap in AI output and create a clear separation between teams that adopt this work pattern and those that don’t.

- [岗位涨跌：科技招聘需求分化 / Job Market Shifts: Diverging Tech Hiring Demand](./sources/articles/job-market-shifts-diverging-tech-hiring-demand.md) — 帖文称，过去一年中前端、移动端、UX 研究与产品设计岗位开放职位下降，而 AI、安全、硬件及前瞻部署工程师岗位上升。 / The post says that over the past year, open roles for front-end, mobile, UX research and product design declined, while AI, security, hardware and forward-deployed engineering roles rose.

- [增长结构：科技招聘回暖，但增长主要来自硬件与基础设施岗位 / Growth Structure: Tech Hiring Recovers, but Growth Comes Mostly from Hardware and Infrastructure Roles](./sources/articles/growth-structure-tech-hiring-recovers-but-growth-comes-mostly-fr.md) — 科技公司空缺回升至近年最好水平，但增长几乎不来自软件岗位，硬件、供应链和数据中心等实体岗位成为主要增量。 / Tech company openings have rebounded to their best level in years, but growth hardly comes from software roles; hardware, supply chain, and data center roles are the main additions.

- [Jim Fan · 真机不加速 / Jim Fan · Real Robot, No Speed-up](./sources/articles/jim-fan-real-robot-no-speed-up.md) — Jim Fan 发布 GR00T 2 在真实灵巧手上执行化学实验并自行清理的端到端实时视频，强调 1x 速度、车载算力运行与开放权重。 / Jim Fan posts GR00T 2 end-to-end real-time video of real dexterous hands performing a chemistry lab experiment and cleaning up, emphasizing 1x speed, onboard compute, and open weights.

- [Mollick · 编程扩散得比预想快 / Mollick · Coding diffusion faster than expected](./sources/articles/mollick-coding-diffusion-faster-than-expected.md) — 伊森·莫利克称编程是AI扩散缓慢的反例，编程专业在真正可用的编程代理出现不到一年后已进入彻底转型，快于历史技术采用率的预期。 / Ethan Mollick says coding is a counterexample to slow AI diffusion, with the profession undergoing complete transformation under a year after real working coding agents, faster than historical adoption rates would suggest.

- [山姆·阿尔特曼警告三星高管：AI需求被重复计算，短期将出现波动 / Sam Altman warns Samsung executives: AI demand double-counted, short-term volatility expected](./sources/articles/sam-altman-warns-samsung-executives-ai-demand-double-counted-sho.md) — OpenAI CEO Sam Altman told Samsung executives that AI memory and data-center demand has been double-counted and may fluctuate in the short term, while long-term demand will surge and chip supply chains face constraints. / OpenAI CEO Sam Altman told Samsung executives that AI memory and data-center demand has been double-counted and may fluctuate in the short term, while long-term demand will surge and chip supply chains face constraints.

- [Google AI Studio 托管智能体支持自托管沙箱环境 / Google AI Studio Managed Agents Support Self-Hosted Sandbox Environments](./sources/articles/google-ai-studio-managed-agents-support-self-hosted-sandbox-envi.md) — Google AI Studio 面向 Gemini API 的托管智能体现已支持自带自托管环境作为沙箱。 / Google AI Studio's managed agents on the Gemini API now support bringing your own self-hosted environment as a sandbox.

- [推出面向医疗保健的 Gemini / Introducing Gemini for healthcare](./sources/articles/introducing-gemini-for-healthcare.md) — Google Cloud 宣布推出面向医疗保健的 Gemini，扩展通用 Gemini 智能体，用于编排临床、财务和行政工作流，并支持 HIPAA 合规。 / Google Cloud announces Gemini for healthcare, extending the universal Gemini agent to orchestrate clinical, financial, and administrative workflows with HIPAA compliance support.

- [A2UI 1.0：构建愉悦、快速、可靠的智能体体验 / A2UI 1.0: Build delightful, fast, reliable, agentic experiences](./sources/articles/a2ui-1-0-build-delightful-fast-reliable-agentic-experiences.md) — Google 发布 A2UI 1.0，首个稳定版生成式 UI 开放协议，让 AI 智能体能以交互界面而非纯文本回复用户。 / Google releases A2UI 1.0, the first stable open protocol for generative UI, enabling AI agents to reply with interactive interfaces instead of plain text.

- [A2UI 1.0 发布：跨平台生成式 UI 开放协议与框架 / A2UI 1.0 Released: Open Protocol and Framework for Generative UI](./sources/articles/a2ui-1-0-released-open-protocol-and-framework-for-generative-ui.md) — Google Cloud 发布 A2UI 1.0，面向 Web、Android、iOS、可穿戴设备、电视等多平台的生成式 UI 开放协议与框架。 / Google Cloud releases A2UI 1.0, an open protocol and framework for generative UI across web, Android, iOS, wearables, TVs, and other surfaces.

- [AlphaGenome 2：解读细胞如何读取 DNA 以更好地理解疾病 / AlphaGenome 2: Deciphering how cells read DNA to better understand disease](./sources/articles/alphagenome-2-deciphering-how-cells-read-dna-to-better-understan.md) — DeepMind 发布下一代 DNA 序列模型 AlphaGenome 2，可预测遗传变异对数百种人类细胞类型的影响，帮助揭示疾病的细胞驱动因素。 / DeepMind releases next-generation DNA sequence model AlphaGenome 2, predicting how genetic variants affect hundreds of human cell types to help uncover cellular drivers of disease.

- [Google DeepMind 发布 AlphaGenome 2，聚焦特定细胞以理解疾病成因 / Google DeepMind releases AlphaGenome 2, focusing on specific cells to understand disease causes](./sources/articles/google-deepmind-releases-alphagenome-2-focusing-on-specific-cell.md) — Google DeepMind 宣布推出 AlphaGenome 2 AI 模型，帮助科学家探究为何 DNA 单字母变化仅在部分细胞中引发疾病。 / Google DeepMind announces AlphaGenome 2, an AI model to help scientists explore why single-letter DNA changes trigger disease in only some cells.

- [Sundar Pichai 发布 AlphaGenome 2，助力揭示疾病细胞驱动因素 / Sundar Pichai Announces AlphaGenome 2 to Uncover Cellular Drivers of Disease](./sources/articles/sundar-pichai-announces-alphagenome-2-to-uncover-cellular-driver.md) — 帖文称 Google DeepMind 的 AlphaGenome 2 可预测 DNA 变异对特定细胞类型的影响，帮助研究人员将遗传发现与疾病根因联系起来。 / The post says Google DeepMind's AlphaGenome 2 can predict how DNA variants affect specific cell types, helping researchers link genetic findings to disease root causes.

- [Demis Hassabis 宣布 AlphaGenome 2 DNA 序列模型 / Demis Hassabis Announces AlphaGenome 2 DNA Sequence Model](./sources/articles/demis-hassabis-announces-alphagenome-2-dna-sequence-model.md) — Demis Hassabis 宣布 AlphaGenome 2 可预测 DNA 变异在特定细胞类型中的影响，供学术研究通过 API 使用。 / Demis Hassabis announces AlphaGenome 2, a DNA sequence model predicting variant impacts in specific cell types, available for academic research via API.

- [Google 推出 Gemini Decisions API：不生成文字只做判断，单次最多 1000 个问题 / Google Launches Gemini Decisions API: Judgment-Only, Up to 1,000 Questions Per Request](./sources/articles/google-launches-gemini-decisions-api-judgment-only-up-to-1-000-q.md) — Google 推出仅输出判断与置信度的 Gemini Decisions API，输入按量计费、输出免费，单次支持最多 1000 个问题。 / Google launches Gemini Decisions API, outputting only judgments and confidence scores; input is metered, output is free, supporting up to 1,000 questions per request.

- [Gemini Decisions API：快速、校准的多模态决策 / Gemini Decisions API: Fast, calibrated multimodal decisions](./sources/articles/gemini-decisions-api-fast-calibrated-multimodal-decisions.md) — Google 推出由 Gemini Decision 1 驱动的 Gemini Decisions API，支持在单次多模态请求中对多达 1,000 个是/否、选择和评分问题进行快速概率决策。 / Google launches the Gemini Decisions API, powered by Gemini Decision 1, for fast probabilistic decisions across up to 1,000 yes/no, choice, and rubric questions in a single multimodal pass.

- [Google AI Studio 发布 Gemini Decisions API / Google AI Studio Launches Gemini Decisions API](./sources/articles/google-ai-studio-launches-gemini-decisions-api.md) — Google AI Studio 推出由 Gemini Decision 1 驱动的 Gemini Decisions API，支持一次处理多达 1000 个跨模态问题。 / Google AI Studio launches the Gemini Decisions API powered by Gemini Decision 1, supporting up to 1000 multimodal questions at once.

- [Gemini Decision 1 发布：每百万输入 tokens 0.10 美元 / Gemini Decision 1 Launched: $0.10 per 1M Input Tokens](./sources/articles/gemini-decision-1-launched-0-10-per-1m-input-tokens.md) — Logan Kilpatrick 宣布 Google DeepMind 的 Gemini Decision 1 前沿决策模型进入 Gemini API，原生支持多模态输入并提供 256K 上下文窗口。 / Logan Kilpatrick announces Google DeepMind's Gemini Decision 1 frontier decision model enters the Gemini API, natively supporting multimodal input with a 256K context window.

- [Sundar Pichai 发布 Gemini Decisions API：单轮评分最高 1000 个问题，输出 Token 免费 / Sundar Pichai Launches Gemini Decisions API: Scores Up to 1,000 Questions in One Pass, Output Tokens Free](./sources/articles/sundar-pichai-launches-gemini-decisions-api-scores-up-to-1-000-q.md) — Sundar Pichai 宣布 Google 推出 Gemini Decisions API，支持文本、图像、音频、视频和 PDF 的闭集选择评分，输入 Token 0.10 美元/百万且输出 Token 不计费。 / Sundar Pichai announces Google's Gemini Decisions API for closed-set choice scoring across text, images, audio, video, and PDFs, priced at $0.10 per 1M input tokens with free output tokens.

- [Eian 发布 Opus 5.5 与 Blender 制作的上海地铁粘土动画 / Eian shares Opus 5.5 and Blender claymation of Shanghai Metro construction](./sources/articles/eian-shares-opus-5-5-and-blender-claymation-of-shanghai-metro-co.md) — Eian 在 X 上发布使用 Opus 5.5 和 Blender 制作的上海全市粘土风格航拍，直观展示上海地铁建设历程。 / Eian posts on X a clay-style aerial view of all Shanghai made with Opus 5.5 and Blender, showing the history of Shanghai Metro construction.

- [通义千问 Token Plan 个人版定价页的标示价格 / Stated prices on the Qwen Token Plan personal pricing page](./sources/articles/alibaba-cloud-qwen-token-plan-personal-pricing.md) — 通义千问平台 Token Plan 个人版页面标明日常价和限时新客折扣，并列出 Lite、Essential、Standard、Pro 四档；页面自己写明最终价格以登录后实际结算为准。 / The Qwen Token Plan personal page states regular prices and a limited new-customer discount, and lists Lite, Essential, Standard, and Pro tiers; the page itself says the final price is whatever is billed after login.

- [斯坦福 CS329Z 讲座视频链接，但抓取页没有正文 / Stanford CS329Z lecture video link, but the fetched page has no body](./sources/articles/stanford-cs329z.md) — 当日精选把这条 YouTube 链接标为斯坦福 CS329Z 讲座入口，但抓取到的页面只有 YouTube 页脚导航，没有视频标题、描述或转录。 / The daily digest marks this YouTube URL as a Stanford CS329Z lecture entry, but the fetched page contains only YouTube footer navigation and no video title, description, or transcript.

- [FrontierHarness v1.1：21 套 Harness × 5 模型 × 62 任务，规模扩大 72 倍 / FrontierHarness v1.1: 21 Harnesses × 5 Models × 62 Tasks, 72× Scale-Up](./sources/articles/frontierharness-v1-1-21-harnesses-5-models-62-tasks-72-scale-up.md) — 帖文称 FrontierHarness v1.1 将投入最高 100B Token，在 21 套 Agent Harness、5 个模型和 62 项任务上测试切换模型时是否也需更换 Harness。 / The post says FrontierHarness v1.1 will spend up to 100B tokens across 21 agent harnesses, 5 models, and 62 tasks to test whether switching models also requires switching harnesses.

- [Enconvo 2.5.13 发布：Mac AI 智能体最大更新 / Enconvo 2.5.13 Released: Biggest Update for Mac AI Agent](./sources/articles/enconvo-2-5-13-released-biggest-update-for-mac-ai-agent.md) — Enconvo AI 发布 2.5.13 版本，推出 Spaces、工作区、可拨打电话的智能体、SSH 主机、可引导子智能体及不接触对话的 API 密钥。 / Enconvo AI releases version 2.5.13, launching Spaces, Workspaces, phone-calling agents, SSH hosts, steerable subagents, and API keys that never touch the chat.

- [阿里拟分拆平头哥赴港上市 / Alibaba Plans to Spin Off Pingtouge for Hong Kong IPO](./sources/articles/alibaba-plans-to-spin-off-pingtouge-for-hong-kong-ipo.md) — 帖文称阿里巴巴拟分拆芯片子公司平头哥在香港独立上市，上市后平头哥仍将是其子公司。 / The post says Alibaba plans to spin off chip subsidiary Pingtouge for an independent Hong Kong listing, with Pingtouge remaining its subsidiary after listing.

- [进度板：长任务前先部署仅构建仪表盘的子 Agent / Progress Dashboard: Deploy a Dashboard-Only Subagent Before Long Tasks](./sources/articles/progress-dashboard-deploy-a-dashboard-only-subagent-before-long.md) — 主张在长任务前先让专用子 Agent 构建自刷新 HTML 进度看板，再由主会话继续编码。 / The post urges launching a dedicated subagent to build a self-refreshing HTML progress dashboard before long tasks, while the main session keeps coding.

- [用 Fable 5.2 为工程师写可直接交付的任务简报 / Write Engineer-Ready Task Briefs with Fable 5.2](./sources/articles/write-engineer-ready-task-briefs-with-fable-5-2.md) — 帖文主张用 Fable 5.2 撰写可直接交给工程师的任务简报，而非要求其优化提示词。 / The post argues for using Fable 5.2 to write task briefs that can be handed directly to engineers, rather than asking it to optimize prompts.

- [分工：昂贵模型规划，子 Agent 执行 / Division of Labor: Expensive Model Plans, Subagents Execute](./sources/articles/division-of-labor-expensive-model-plans-subagents-execute.md) — 帖文称，用提示词让昂贵的 Fable 5.2 只在规划与洞察上烧 Token，将实现分派给 Opus 5.5 子 Agent 或 Codex，并以红队审查挑战计划。 / The post claims a prompt keeps costly Fable 5.2 burning tokens only on planning and insight, dispatching implementation to Opus 5.5 subagents or Codex, with red-team review to challenge the plan.

- [Matt · 主仓库 / Matt · Home Repo](./sources/articles/matt-home-repo.md) — 用一个“主仓库”统一管理技能、指令与工具，在 AGENTS.md 中指向多个代码仓，让 Agent 跨仓协作。 / Use a single “home repo” to unify skills, instructions, and tooling, with AGENTS.md pointing to multiple repos so agents can work across repositories.

- [Simon · 「这仓库是你的」 / Simon · "This codebase is yours"](./sources/articles/simon-this-codebase-is-yours.md) — Simon Willison 在 X 上发帖称，Anthropic 发布新模型时值得查看其更新的提示词指南，并分享让 Fable 5.2 在改进现有代码时更少谨慎的诀窍：告诉它“这仓库是你的”。 / Simon Willison posted on X that Anthropic's updated prompting guide is worth checking with new model releases, and shared a tip to make Fable 5.2 less cautious when improving existing code: tell it "This codebase is yours".

- [纳森·兰伯特：AI 扩散依赖人类专家，知识工作比评测更具人性 / Nathan Lambert: AI Diffusion Depends on Human Experts, Knowledge Work Is More Human Than Evals Suggest](./sources/articles/nathan-lambert-ai-diffusion-depends-on-human-experts-knowledge-w.md) — 纳森·兰伯特认为 AI 惠及长尾知识工作需人类专家推动，Mercor 或可居扩散中心。 / Nathan Lambert argues that bringing AI benefits to the long tail of knowledge work requires human experts, with Mercor potentially at the center of diffusion.

- [Manus 招聘：内部不分前后端，面试改用 Manus 实战四小时 / Manus Recruitment: No Frontend/Backend Split Internally, Interviews Use Four-Hour Manus Practice](./sources/articles/manus-recruitment-no-frontend-backend-split-internally-interview.md) — Manus 联合创始人称公司内部已不分前后端等角色，产品经理和设计师也在写代码，面试改为让候选人用 Manus 四小时解决问题并复盘人机协作记录。 / Manus co-founder says the company no longer divides roles such as frontend/backend internally; product managers and designers also write code, and interviews now ask candidates to solve a problem with Manus in four hours and review the human-AI collaboration record.

- [试试 Fable 5.2 的成色 / Testing Fable 5.2's Quality](./sources/articles/testing-fable-5-2-s-quality.md) — 用户在 X 上发帖称要试试 Fable 5.2 的成色，并附带一张图片。 / A user posts on X to test Fable 5.2's quality, attaching an image.

- [Fable 5.2 生成实时水墨流体交互作品 / Fable 5.2 Generates Real-Time Ink Fluid Interactive Work](./sources/articles/fable-5-2-generates-real-time-ink-fluid-interactive-work.md) — 帖文称 Fable 5.2 无需指定主题即自主生成可交互项目：打字生成对应墨迹，墨水为实时流体模拟，配有音效与音乐，物品和天气动态且相互影响。 / The post claims Fable 5.2 autonomously created an interactive project without a specified theme: typing generates corresponding ink imagery, the ink is a real-time fluid simulation with sound effects and music, and objects and weather are dynamic and mutually influential.

- [OpenAI dot 现支持同一账号连接多台本地电脑 / OpenAI dot now supports connecting multiple local computers to one account](./sources/articles/openai-dot-now-supports-connecting-multiple-local-computers-to-o.md) — OpenAI 的 dot AI 助理现允许同一账号连接多台本地电脑，并为每个任务指定使用哪一台。 / OpenAI's dot AI assistant now lets one account connect multiple local computers and assign a specific machine per task.

- [OpenAI 发售 200 美元 Codex 版 Chromatic 掌机，一句话用 AI 做 Game Boy 游戏 / OpenAI Sells $200 Codex Edition Chromatic Handheld, Make Game Boy Games with One Sentence via AI](./sources/articles/openai-sells-200-codex-edition-chromatic-handheld-make-game-boy.md) — OpenAI 与 ModRetro 联合推出 Codex 版 Chromatic 掌机，售价 200 美元，用户通过 Codex 插件用自然语言描述游戏构思，生成可在模拟器或真机运行的 Game Boy ROM。 / OpenAI and ModRetro launch the $200 Codex edition Chromatic handheld, letting users describe game ideas in natural language via the Codex plugin to generate Game Boy ROMs runnable on emulators or the device.

- [OpenAI 开发者宣布 Codex 版 ModRetro Chromatic 上架 Supply Co / OpenAI Developers Announce Codex Edition of ModRetro Chromatic on Supply Co](./sources/articles/openai-developers-announce-codex-edition-of-modretro-chromatic-o.md) — 帖文称，通过 Codex 中的 ModRetro Chromatic 插件，用户可在掌上设备上设计关卡、制作动画精灵并试玩自制游戏。 / The post says that via the ModRetro Chromatic plugin in Codex, users can design levels, animate sprites, and playtest homemade games on handheld devices.

- [代码少即多 / Fewer, Deeper Modules, Smaller Interfaces](./sources/articles/fewer-deeper-modules-smaller-interfaces.md) — 帖文推荐五词代码清理提示词，主张通过更少更深的模块和更小的接口精简代码库。 / The post recommends a five-word code cleanup prompt advocating fewer, deeper modules and smaller interfaces to streamline codebases.

- [一张截图对齐 Claude Code 项目环境 / Align Claude Code project environment with one screenshot](./sources/articles/align-claude-code-project-environment-with-one-screenshot.md) — 帖文称，拖入一张截图即可让 Claude Code 对比当前项目设置并补全缺失项，无需手动逐项配置。 / The post claims that dragging one screenshot into Claude Code lets it compare the current project setup and fill in missing items without manual item-by-item configuration.

- [Kent · 显式状态机 / Kent · Explicit State Machines](./sources/articles/kent-explicit-state-machines.md) — Kent C. Dodds 转发一段提示词，主张找出应用中的隐式状态机并将其显式化，以兼顾用户体验、开发者体验与智能体体验。 / Kent C. Dodds shares a prompt urging developers to find implicit state machines in their apps and make them explicit while balancing UX, DX, and AX.

- [软件工厂：智能体上层的自动化基建 / Software Factory: Automation Infrastructure Above Agents](./sources/articles/software-factory-automation-infrastructure-above-agents.md) — 帖文称，SOTA 团队正从直接用智能体开产品，转向构建帮助智能体开产品的自动化，并称这层基建为“软件工厂”。 / The post says SOTA teams are moving from using agents to build products directly to building automations that help agents build products, calling this layer the “software factory.”

- [为什么要向 AI 代理讲清问题而非方案：一个提升面谈质量的技巧 / Why describe the problem to an AI agent rather than prescribe a solution: one tip for better grilling](./sources/articles/why-describe-the-problem-to-an-ai-agent-rather-than-prescribe-a.md) — 帖文称，向 AI 代理说明问题背景（“为什么”）而非直接给出解决方案（“什么”），能获得更深入、更具创造性的对话。 / The post says explaining the problem context (“why”) to an AI agent instead of prescribing a solution (“what”) leads to deeper, more creative conversations.

- [Simon · PR 折叠：隐藏 AI 生成的 PR 描述 / Simon · PR Fold: Hiding AI-Generated PR Descriptions](./sources/articles/simon-pr-fold-hiding-ai-generated-pr-descriptions.md) — Simon Willison 建议在 GitHub 中用 `<details><summary>` 折叠 AI 生成的 PR 描述，以降低阅读 AI 生成 PR 滥竽充数内容的疲劳感。 / Simon Willison suggests collapsing AI-generated GitHub PR descriptions behind `<details><summary>` to reduce fatigue from reading PR slop.

- [兰伯特期待更多 AI 实验室与 Anthropic 竞争并分发研究能力 / Lambert Expects More AI Labs to Compete with Anthropic and Distribute Research Capabilities](./sources/articles/lambert-expects-more-ai-labs-to-compete-with-anthropic-and-distr.md) — 纳森·兰伯特在 X 上发帖称，期待更多实验室与 Anthropic 竞争，分发 AI 研究能力，并让安全社区基于这些工具构建价值。 / Nathan Lambert posts on X that he expects more labs to compete with Anthropic, distribute AI research capabilities, and let the safety community build value on these tools.

- [Chollet 点赞自治生意：收购 SaaS 交给 Agent Harness 跑 P&L / Chollet Endorses Autonomous Businesses: Acquire SaaS, Hand to Agent Harness, Optimize P&L](./sources/articles/chollet-endorses-autonomous-businesses-acquire-saas-hand-to-agen.md) — François Chollet 称赞 Sam Pasupalak 推出的 AI CEO「Steve」，主张收购停滞 SaaS 并由 Agent Harness 全权运营，以损益表为目标函数。 / François Chollet praises Sam Pasupalak's AI CEO 'Steve', advocating acquiring stagnant SaaS and letting an agent harness run them end-to-end with P&L as the objective.

- [传送门实验 / Portal Experiment](./sources/articles/portal-experiment.md) — 帖文称 Ethan Mollick 指示 Fable 5.2 生成由传送门连接的无尽 3D 世界，产生 2.5 万个智能体与 450 个世界。 / The post says Ethan Mollick instructed Fable 5.2 to generate endless 3D worlds connected by portals, producing 25,000 agents and 450 worlds.

- [帖文称 Fable 5.2 触及“锯齿状 AGI” / Post says Fable 5.2 touches 'Jagged AGI'](./sources/articles/post-says-fable-5-2-touches-jagged-agi.md) — 帖文称 Fable 5.2 能创业、做学术研究、黑客硬件并建 3D 模型，展示“锯齿状 AGI”的能力与局限。 / The post says Fable 5.2 can start a business, conduct academic research, hack hardware, and build 3D models, showing the capabilities and limits of 'Jagged AGI'.

- [Amasad · 自愈软件 / Amasad · Self-Healing Software](./sources/articles/amasad-self-healing-software.md) — Amjad Masad 回应“谁来维护 vibe 编码的应用”，提出自愈软件构想，并关联 Replit 监控 Agent 自动检测、调查、修复并部署生产问题。 / Amjad Masad responds to 'who maintains vibe-coded apps' with self-healing software, citing Replit's Monitoring Agent as a 24/7 system that detects production issues, investigates, validates fixes, and deploys them.

- [Levie：Agent 蜂群将生产力提升推向无上限 / Levie: Agent Swarms Push Productivity Gains Beyond Limits](./sources/articles/levie-agent-swarms-push-productivity-gains-beyond-limits.md) — Aaron Levie 认为 Agent 蜂群把复杂工作扇出到多个 Agent 并行执行，将把生产力从 20–50% 的提升推向没有上限，并深刻改变编程、网络安全与生命科学等领域。 / Aaron Levie argues that agent swarms fanning out complex work across many agents in parallel will push productivity gains from 20–50% to uncapped levels, profoundly changing coding, cybersecurity, and life sciences.

- [宝玉：新模型发布是清理提示词的好时机 / Bao Yu: New model releases are a good time to clean up prompts](./sources/articles/bao-yu-new-model-releases-are-a-good-time-to-clean-up-prompts.md) — 宝玉转发 Alex Albert 建议，每次新模型发布时用提示词审阅整个项目，找出损坏、不一致、低效或不再需要的内容并修复，并高度推荐尝试。 / Bao Yu reposts Alex Albert's suggestion to use a prompt on each new model release to review the whole project and fix broken, inconsistent, inefficient, or unneeded items, highly recommending it.

- [Alex Albert 推荐用同一提示词审查项目 / Alex Albert Recommends Same Prompt to Audit Projects](./sources/articles/alex-albert-recommends-same-prompt-to-audit-projects.md) — 帖文称，每次有新模型时，他在所有项目中运行同一条提示词，找出损坏、不一致、低效或不再需要的内容。 / The post says that whenever a new model arrives, he runs the same prompt across all projects to find broken, inconsistent, inefficient, or unneeded items.

- [OpenAI 推出 Control Room 集中追踪与叫停模型工作负载 / OpenAI Launches Control Room to Centrally Track and Stop Model Workloads](./sources/articles/openai-launches-control-room-to-centrally-track-and-stop-model-w.md) — OpenAI 推出 Control Room，在研究基础设施中集中追踪模型工作负载，并在安全或安全问题出现时快速停止。 / OpenAI launched Control Room to centrally track model workloads across research infrastructure and stop them quickly when safety or security concerns arise.

- [宝玉解读：Meta 发布最小模型 Muse Ember，单价与 Claude Haiku 5.5 一样，同测试花费三分之一 / Meta Releases Smallest Model Muse Ember, Same Unit Price as Claude Haiku 5.5, One-Third the Cost for Same Tests](./sources/articles/meta-releases-smallest-model-muse-ember-same-unit-price-as-claud.md) — 帖文称 Meta 发布最小、最便宜的模型 Muse Ember，主打 AI Agent 重复杂活，单价与 Claude Haiku 5.5 相同，跑同一套测试仅花三分之一费用。 / The post says Meta released its smallest and cheapest model Muse Ember, targeting repetitive agent tasks, with unit pricing matching Claude Haiku 5.5 and one-third the cost for the same test suite.

- [Muse Ember 上线 Meta Model API，输入输出 Token 定价公布 / Muse Ember launches on Meta Model API with input/output token pricing](./sources/articles/muse-ember-launches-on-meta-model-api-with-input-output-token-pr.md) — Meta 宣布其最小、最高效模型 Muse Ember 现已在 Meta Model API 上线，输入每百万代币 0.10 美元，输出每百万代币 0.50 美元。 / Meta announces its smallest, most efficient model Muse Ember is now available on the Meta Model API, priced at $0.10 per million input tokens and $0.50 per million output tokens.

- [Muse Ember 成本对比：每任务成本约为 Claude Haiku 5.5 三分之一 / Muse Ember cost comparison: roughly one-third the cost per task of Claude Haiku 5.5](./sources/articles/muse-ember-cost-comparison-roughly-one-third-the-cost-per-task-o.md) — 帖文称 Meta 的 Muse Ember 在人工智能分析智能指数上与 Claude Haiku 5.5 性能相当，但每任务成本仅约三分之一，且在 GPT-6 Luna 的所有推理设置中得分更高。 / The post claims Meta's Muse Ember matches Claude Haiku 5.5 on the Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index at about one-third the cost per task, and scores higher than GPT-6 Luna across all Luna reasoning settings.

- [Meta 发布 Muse Ember 小型高效 Agent 模型 / Meta Releases Muse Ember, a Small and Efficient Agent Model](./sources/articles/meta-releases-muse-ember-a-small-and-efficient-agent-model.md) — 帖文称 Meta 推出 Muse Ember，定位为最小且最高效的模型，用于复杂推理执行与日常 Agent 工作。 / The post says Meta launched Muse Ember, positioned as its smallest and most efficient model for complex reasoning execution and everyday agent work.

- [Boris Cherny：帖文称 Fable 5.2 订阅越久重置次数越多 / Boris Cherny: Post says Fable 5.2 grants more resets to longer-tenured subscribers](./sources/articles/boris-cherny-post-says-fable-5-2-grants-more-resets-to-longer-te.md) — 帖文称 Fable 5.2 会按订阅时长向老用户发放更多重置次数，页面还展示了 Claude Fable 5.2 与 Claude Mythos 5.2 的介绍。 / The post says Fable 5.2 grants more resets to longer-tenured subscribers, and the page also shows an introduction to Claude Fable 5.2 and Claude Mythos 5.2.

- [Anthropic 转发 Claude Fable 5.2 与 Claude Mythos 5.2 上线 / Anthropic Boosts Claude Fable 5.2 and Claude Mythos 5.2 Release](./sources/articles/anthropic-boosts-claude-fable-5-2-and-claude-mythos-5-2-release.md) — Anthropic 转发 @claudeai 帖文，称 Claude Fable 5.2 和 Claude Mythos 5.2 现已可用，旨在应对艰巨技术挑战，并在编程、推理和视觉等方面改进。 / Anthropic boosts @claudeai's post stating Claude Fable 5.2 and Claude Mythos 5.2 are now available, designed for daunting technical challenges with improvements in coding, reasoning, and vision.

- [Claude Fable 5.2 从一句话设计电子设备 / Claude Fable 5.2 designs electronic devices from a one-paragraph prompt](./sources/articles/claude-fable-5-2-designs-electronic-devices-from-a-one-paragraph.md) — 帖文称 Claude Fable 5.2 在预发布测试中，仅凭一段简短提示词就设计出三款可工作的电子设备，包括家用语音助手中枢。 / The post claims Claude Fable 5.2 designed three working electronic devices from a one-paragraph prompt during pre-release testing, including a home voice assistant hub.

- [Claude Fable 5.2 与 Claude Mythos 5.2 上线 / Claude Fable 5.2 and Claude Mythos 5.2 Launch](./sources/articles/claude-fable-5-2-and-claude-mythos-5-2-launch.md) — Anthropic 官方 X 账号宣布推出 Claude Fable 5.2 与 Claude Mythos 5.2，帖文称其用于应对艰巨技术挑战，并在编程、推理与视觉等方面取得进步。 / Anthropic's official X account announces Claude Fable 5.2 and Claude Mythos 5.2, stating they are designed for daunting technical challenges and show improvements in coding, reasoning, and vision.

- [GitHub Lever：面向编程 Agent 的反馈循环 Copilot 插件 / GitHub Lever: Feedback-loop Copilot Plugin for Coding Agents](./sources/articles/github-lever-feedback-loop-copilot-plugin-for-coding-agents.md) — GitHub 推出的 Copilot 插件 lever，让编程 Agent 通过循环卡片自行检查工作并持续迭代改进。 / GitHub's Copilot plugin lever lets coding agents check their own work and iterate continuously using Loop Cards.

- [Gojo 官网：面向 Agent 的内存安全系统语言 / Gojo Official Website: Memory-Safe Systems Language for Agents](./sources/articles/gojo-official-website-memory-safe-systems-language-for-agents.md) — Gojo 是一款面向编程代理的内存安全系统编程语言，无需标注生命周期，错误即值，编译为 C 并生成单个原生二进制。 / Gojo is a memory-safe systems programming language for coding agents, requiring no lifetime annotations, treating errors as values, and compiling to C then a single native binary.

- [卡帕蒂用 Claude Code 三分钟做出 TristanTube，两小时过滤出 HackerTube / Karpathy Builds TristanTube in Three Minutes with Claude Code and Filters HackerTube in Two Hours](./sources/articles/karpathy-builds-tristantube-in-three-minutes-with-claude-code-an.md) — 帖文称卡帕蒂用 Claude Code 一键生成收录约 4.7 万条视频的 TristanTube，并用 Claude Opus 5.5 把 20 年约 4300 万条 Hacker News 评论筛选为 6454 个视频的 HackerTube。 / The post says Karpathy one-shot generated TristanTube with Claude Code, collecting about 47,000 videos, and used Claude Opus 5.5 to filter about 43 million Hacker News comments over 20 years into 6,454 videos for HackerTube.

- [数据与算力只是乘数，更聪明的 AI 方法才能更好地扩展 / Data and Compute Are Only Multipliers; Cleverer AI Methods Scale Better](./sources/articles/data-and-compute-are-only-multipliers-cleverer-ai-methods-scale.md) — 帖文称，数据规模和算力规模只是泛化能力的乘数，更聪明的方法才能更好地扩展。 / The post says data and compute scaling are only multipliers of generalization power, and cleverer approaches scale better.

- [艾森·莫利克称近期 AI 模型事实核查能力超越人类专家 / Ethan Mollick Says Recent AI Models Surpass Human Experts in Fact-Checking](./sources/articles/ethan-mollick-says-recent-ai-models-surpass-human-experts-in-fac.md) — 帖文称近期 AI 模型在查找事实和核查声明方面已优于包括博士专家在内的人类专家，日常工作中对 AI 输出事实核验的担忧过多。 / The post argues that concerns about verifying AI output for factual errors in everyday work are excessive, citing recent models that outperform human experts, including PhD specialists, at finding facts and checking claims.

- [世界一下就切换到了禁止 AI 的时间线 / The World Suddenly Switched to a Timeline Banning AI](./sources/articles/the-world-suddenly-switched-to-a-timeline-banning-ai.md) — 帖文称美国民主党议员提议暂停人工超级智能研发，引发市场恐慌，并讨论 AI 监管、算力泡沫与普通人未获技术红利等问题。 / The post claims a US Democratic lawmaker proposed suspending artificial superintelligence development, triggering market panic and discussion on AI regulation, compute bubbles, and ordinary people not receiving AI dividends.

- [《世界一下就切换到了禁止 AI 的时间线》—— 橘 AI 对超级 AI 禁令的理性复盘 / "World Switched to AI-Ban Timeline Overnight" — Orange AI's Rational Take on Super-AI Prohibition](./sources/articles/world-switched-to-ai-ban-timeline-overnight-orange-ai-s-rational.md) — 橘 AI 作者针对近期从 AI 基建狂热反转为超级 AI 禁令的现象，提出三点核心观点：大力发展 AI 但不制造泡沫、重视超级 AI 安全、区分当前替代价值与未来增量价值。 / Orange AI author responds to the sudden shift from AI infrastructure mania to super-AI bans with three tenets: develop AI vigorously without blowing bubbles, prioritize super-AI safety, and distinguish today's replacement value from tomorrow's incremental value.

- [Claude 会做动画，不代表每个 UI 元素都要加动画 / Claude Can Animate, But Not Every UI Element Needs Animation](./sources/articles/claude-can-animate-but-not-every-ui-element-needs-animation.md) — 小邓同学在 X 上吐槽：Claude 会做动画，不代表每个按钮、卡片和页面切换都要加花哨动画。 / Xiaodong Xue complains on X that just because Claude can create animations does not mean every button, card, and page transition needs flashy animation.

- [旗舰晚发或许在抢规则权 / Delayed Flagship Releases May Be a Bid for Rule-Making Power](./sources/articles/delayed-flagship-releases-may-be-a-bid-for-rule-making-power.md) — 帖文称 OpenAI 与 Anthropic 推迟通用旗舰模型，或在借安全议题与受控发布争夺 AI 行业规则制定权。 / The post claims OpenAI and Anthropic are delaying general flagship models, possibly competing for AI industry rule-making power through safety issues and controlled releases.

- [很多 AI Agent 本质是绑信用卡的 cron job / Many AI Agents Are Essentially Cron Jobs with Credit Cards](./sources/articles/many-ai-agents-are-essentially-cron-jobs-with-credit-cards.md) — 研究者调查 3.6 万 GitHub 仓库发现：Agent 触发器众多却缺检查与退出机制，不知任务对错却懂持续扣费。 / Study of 36k GitHub repos finds agents have many triggers but few checks/exits — they don't know if tasks succeed but know how to keep charging.

- [对 AI 内容疲劳，想转当吃喝视频博主 / Tired of AI content, wants to become a food and drink vlogger](./sources/articles/tired-of-ai-content-wants-to-become-a-food-and-drink-vlogger.md) — 用户在 X 发帖称厌倦满屏 AI，想像隋坡一样做全国吃喝视频博主，并好奇小时候学相声或当厨子的人生会如何。 / The user posts on X saying they are tired of AI-filled screens and want to become a food vlogger like Suibo, wondering what life would be like if they had learned crosstalk or become a chef.

- [推荐 Lattice：本地 LaTeX 科研工作区 / Recommending Lattice: A Local LaTeX Research Workspace](./sources/articles/recommending-lattice-a-local-latex-research-workspace.md) — 帖文称 Lattice 是面向科研的本地 LaTeX 工作区，整合论文、笔记、幻灯片、Overleaf 协作者与 AI 代理，免费开源下载。 / The post recommends Lattice, a free open-source local LaTeX workspace for research that integrates papers, notes, slides, Overleaf collaborators, and an AI agent.

- [今后 A 社可能不会封号了 / Post claims company A may stop banning accounts](./sources/articles/post-claims-company-a-may-stop-banning-accounts.md) — 帖文称 OpenAI 近期出现大规模封号，并将此与某员工此前跳槽 Cursor 后限制中国用户使用 Claude 联系起来。 / The post claims OpenAI recently saw mass account bans and links them to an employee who previously joined Cursor, after which China users were restricted from using Claude.

- [ego-lite 跑报销：浏览器 Agent 自动化费用报销 / ego-lite Runs Expense Reimbursement: Browser Agent Automates Fee Process](./sources/articles/ego-lite-runs-expense-reimbursement-browser-agent-automates-fee.md) — 帖文称 ego-lite 的 /ego-browser 技能可让 Codex、Claude Code、Cursor 等 Agent 自动完成合并发票、填报销单、上传附件等报销流程。 / The post claims ego-lite's /ego-browser skill lets agents such as Codex, Claude Code, and Cursor automatically complete expense reimbursement steps including merging invoices, filling forms, and uploading attachments.

- [Grok Bot 浏览器插件通过 Chrome 应用商店审核 / Grok Bot browser extension passes Chrome Web Store review](./sources/articles/grok-bot-browser-extension-passes-chrome-web-store-review.md) — 铁柱AGI 的 Grok Bot 浏览器插件已通过 Chrome 审核，支持将网页截图、元素、整页及 X、小红书、抖音、公众号内容发送至指定 Grok Bot。 / Tiezhu AGI's Grok Bot browser extension has passed Chrome review, supporting sending webpage screenshots, elements, full pages, and X, Xiaohongshu, Douyin, and Official Account content to specified Grok Bots.

- [Uber Agent 安全实践：有权限执行，不代表符合用户本意 / Uber Agent Security Practice: Permission to Execute Does Not Mean It Matches User Intent](./sources/articles/uber-agent-security-practice-permission-to-execute-does-not-mean.md) — Uber Secure AI 通过上线前审查、运行时约束和持续测试，核对智能体动作是否真正源自用户请求，而非仅依赖传统权限，但未公布量化防护数据。 / Uber Secure AI verifies whether agent actions truly originate from user requests through pre-deployment review, runtime constraints, and continuous testing, rather than relying solely on traditional permissions, without publishing quantitative protection data.

- [Airbnb 小模型以约 1% 推理成本匹敌前沿模型分类水平 / Airbnb Small Model Matches Frontier Model Classification at ~1% Inference Cost](./sources/articles/airbnb-small-model-matches-frontier-model-classification-at-1-in.md) — Airbnb 实测显示，针对高频内容审核任务微调的约 20 亿参数小模型，分类效果接近前沿模型，延迟低于 100 毫秒，高流量下推理成本约为前沿 API 的 1%。 / Airbnb's real-world test shows a fine-tuned ~2B-parameter small model matches frontier model classification for high-frequency content moderation, with sub-100ms latency and about 1% of frontier API inference cost at high traffic.

- [强制 Responses API / Forced Responses API](./sources/articles/forced-responses-api.md) — 帖文称 magpie v0.1.3157 让分组只要有一名成员适合 Responses，整组就走 Responses，不再因支持 Chat 的中转被拉回 Chat。 / The post says magpie v0.1.3157 makes a group use Responses API if any member suits it, so it is no longer pulled back to Chat by a Chat-supporting relay.

- [magpie 将额度用尽原样透传给 Codex / magpie passes ChatGPT quota-exhausted status through to Codex](./sources/articles/magpie-passes-chatgpt-quota-exhausted-status-through-to-codex.md) — 帖文称 magpie v0.1.3160 已修复多个 ChatGPT 账号额度用尽时的处理，不再将官方“额度用尽”改写为普通 429，而是原样透给 Codex 以显示额度和重置时间。 / The post says magpie v0.1.3160 now passes the official “quota exhausted” status through to Codex instead of rewriting it as a generic 429, so Codex shows quota and reset time.

- [Motion Prompt：建立 AI 动效与镜头标准词典 / Motion Prompt: A Standard Dictionary for AI Motion Effects and Camera Work](./sources/articles/motion-prompt-a-standard-dictionary-for-ai-motion-effects-and-ca.md) — 帖文介绍 Motion Prompt 项目，通过 192 个带演示与提示词示例的专业词汇及配套插件，帮助创作者精确指挥 AI 生成动效与镜头。 / The post introduces Motion Prompt, a project using 192 demonstrated terms with prompt examples and a companion plugin to help creators precisely direct AI-generated motion effects and camera work.

- [StudyOnTV：答题通关才能看电视的开源屏幕管理工具 / StudyOnTV: Open-source screen manager that unlocks TV time after quiz completion](./sources/articles/studyontv-open-source-screen-manager-that-unlocks-tv-time-after.md) — 一款基于 Kotlin 的开源 Android 应用，孩子需先答对题目才能赚取屏幕使用时间，数据存在设备本地，支持接入 DeepSeek 等 AI 模型。 / An open-source Kotlin-based Android app that requires children to answer questions correctly before earning screen time, stores data locally, and supports AI models such as DeepSeek.

- [Inkling 2 Small：西方开源的“像模像样”模型 / Inkling 2 Small: A Decent Western Open-Source Model](./sources/articles/inkling-2-small-a-decent-western-open-source-model.md) — 帖文称 Inkling 2 Small 总参数 594B、激活参数 17B，成绩接近 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash-1017 并超过 GLM 5.3 Flash，模型下周开源。 / The post says Inkling 2 Small has 594B total and 17B active parameters, scores close to DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash-1017 and exceeds GLM 5.3 Flash, and the model will be open-sourced next week.

- [数据公司研究影响力投票 / Data Company Research Influence Vote](./sources/articles/data-company-research-influence-vote.md) — 帖文称，50位业内人士投票评选影响前沿AI研究方向的数据公司，Surge以16票居首，Proximal以13票紧随其后，Scale、Mercor等大公司并未全面领先。 / The post says 50 industry professionals voted on which data companies influence frontier AI research direction, with Surge leading at 16 votes and Proximal second at 13, while large firms like Scale and Mercor did not lead comprehensively.

- [AI 网安任务跨度一年扩大 52 倍，漏洞利用加速但修补跟不上 / AI Cyber Task Horizon Grows 52× in a Year; Exploitation Outpaces Patching](./sources/articles/ai-cyber-task-horizon-grows-52-in-a-year-exploitation-outpaces-p.md) — 帖文称 AI 网络攻防能力按代码能力曲线每 4-5 个月翻倍，最长可靠任务从 5.5 分钟增至 4.8 小时，漏洞利用加速而修补滞后。 / Post reports AI cyber offense/defense doubles every 4–5 months per code-capability curve; longest reliable task grew from 5.5 min to 4.8 hrs, while exploitation accelerates but patching lags.

- [别再盯着 40 个 AI 基准，应问 Agent 能否自主把活干完 / Stop Watching the 40 AI Benchmarks; Ask Whether Agents Can Actually Get the Work Done](./sources/articles/stop-watching-the-40-ai-benchmarks-ask-whether-agents-can-actual.md) — 帖文引用 Palisade Research 的 Jeff Ladish 称，应以真实有经济价值的工作替代 40 个 AI 基准，考察 Agent 能否自主完成任务。 / The post cites Palisade Research's Jeff Ladish saying real economically valuable work should replace 40 AI benchmarks to test whether agents can autonomously complete tasks.

- [Vox：Claude Code 子 Agent 状态更新费用下降 99%，附三项省额度设置 / Vox: Claude Code background subagent status update costs cut 99%, plus three usage-limit settings](./sources/articles/vox-claude-code-background-subagent-status-update-costs-cut-99-p.md) — Anthropic 大幅降低 Claude Code 后台子 Agent 状态更新费用，官方文档还提供三项可调设置以进一步节省使用额度。 / Anthropic has slashed Claude Code background subagent status update costs by 99%, and the official docs offer three more settings to further stretch usage limits.

- [VFlash：并行视觉模型开启 System One 时代，面向物理 AI / VFlash: System One Vision Model Era for Physical AI](./sources/articles/vflash-system-one-vision-model-era-for-physical-ai.md) — Jim Fan 发布 VFlash，通过一次性输出整体几何形状而非逐 token 解码，实现物理 AI 任务的高速并行推理。 / Jim Fan introduces VFlash, a System One vision model that outputs entire geometric shapes in parallel instead of token-by-token decoding for fast physical AI inference.

- [纳森·兰伯特称基准不足是 RSI 瓶颈，METR 发布 AI 加速 AI 开发风险评估 / Nathan Lambert says benchmark gaps bottleneck RSI; METR releases AI-accelerating-AI risk assessment](./sources/articles/nathan-lambert-says-benchmark-gaps-bottleneck-rsi-metr-releases.md) — 帖文称优质基准不足是递归自我改进的瓶颈，现有基准可降低模型成本；METR 正发布关于 AI 是否加速 AI 开发的风险评估报告。 / The post says insufficient good benchmarks bottleneck recursive self-improvement, while existing benchmarks can cut model costs; METR is publishing a risk assessment report on whether AI is accelerating AI development.

- [企业 AI 转型内部的两种力量 / Two Forces Inside Corporate AI Transformation](./sources/articles/two-forces-inside-corporate-ai-transformation.md) — 艾森·莫利克指出，采用 AI 的企业内部存在两种自称推动 AI 转型的力量：一派视 AI 为生存威胁或机遇，另一派希望将其视为常规技术以维持现状。 / Ethan Mollick notes that inside every firm adopting AI are two forces both claiming to work on AI transformation: those who see AI as an existential threat or opportunity, and those who want it treated as a regular technology so nothing changes.

Agents Evolve Codebases as Apple Cuts Budgets](./sources/articles/do-individual-ai-limits-still-make-sense-agents-evolve-codebases.md) — 彼得·施泰因伯格质疑个体 AI 使用限额的意义，并转述苹果削减员工 AI 预算的报道。 / Peter Steinberger questions the relevance of individual AI usage limits and relays reports of Apple cutting employee AI budgets.

- [AI 将重写所有软件，标志软件工程真正诞生 / AI Will Rewrite All Software, Marking the True Birth of Software Engineering](./sources/articles/ai-will-rewrite-all-software-marking-the-true-birth-of-software.md) — 格雷姆·劳奇预言 AI 将重写缓慢、漏洞众多且规格不足的人造软件，使其快速、安全并经过形式化验证。 / Guillermo Rauch predicts AI will rewrite slow, vulnerable, underspecified human-made software, making it fast, secure, and formally verified.

- [逆向 Design Skill：先示例后诱导，绕过直接询问系统提示词 / Reverse-Engineering Claude's Design Skill: Demonstrate First, Then Induce, Avoiding Direct System-Prompt Requests](./sources/articles/reverse-engineering-claude-s-design-skill-demonstrate-first-then.md) — 帖文称，不直接问 Claude 索取系统提示词，而是先让它用 Design Skill 做简单设计，再要求生成输入输出一致的同类 Skill，即可大体还原原版技能。 / The post claims that instead of directly asking Claude for its system prompt, users should first have it complete a simple design using its Design Skill, then request a similar Skill with identical input/output, roughly reproducing the original.
